cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(spmv_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(spmv spmv.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(spmv PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(spmv m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;

    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Allocate and initialize data structures
    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector

    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

    // For validation, compute reference solution
    std::vector<double> h_reference;
    if (validate) {
        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
        h_reference.resize(numRows);
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
    }

    // Perform SpMV computation
    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());
    }

    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);
    
    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);

        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

    }

void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {

    srand(8675309);

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;

    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Allocate and initialize data structures
    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector

    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

    // For validation, compute reference solution
    std::vector<double> h_reference;
    if (validate) {
        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
        h_reference.resize(numRows);
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
    }

    // Perform SpMV computation
    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());
    }

    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);
    
    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);

        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;

exec

codex
The benchmark reuses the same matrix and input vector on every iteration. I’ll distribute complete CSR rows, balance partitions by nonzero count and row overhead, and broadcast the vector once. That keeps communication out of the repeated computation and preserves each row’s summation order. Rank zero will retain the original initialization sequence so results can be compared directly.

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}

+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+// Row delimiter slices may overlap by one element when scattering.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
+            : local;

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;

+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+// Row delimiter slices may overlap by one element when scattering.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+
+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+        printf("Too many non-zero elements for 32-bit CSR indices.\n");
+        return 1;
+    }
+    return 0;

+
+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            const uint64_t numEntriesLeft = static_cast<uint64_t>(dim) * dim
                                          - (static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * dim + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

struct Options {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
};

bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    errno = 0;
    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
        return false;
    }
    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
    return true;
}

// Only rank zero parses and prints; broadcast the outcome before any data transfer.
int parseOptions(int argc, char** argv, Options& options) {
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], options.numRows)) return 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], options.sparsity)) return 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], options.iterations)) return 1;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            options.maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            options.validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            options.printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 2;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    if (options.sparsity == 0) {
        printf("Sparsity must be greater than zero.\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const uint64_t nItems = static_cast<uint64_t>(options.numRows) * options.numRows
                          / options.sparsity;
    if (nItems > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
        printf("Too many non-zero elements for 32-bit CSR indices.\n");
        return 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
    cuts.back() = numRows;
    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
        index_t hi = numRows;
        while (lo < hi) {
            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
                lo = mid + 1;
            } else {
                hi = mid;
            }
        }
        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
        }
        cuts[rank] = lo;
    }
    return cuts;
}

// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
template <typename T>
void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
    uint64_t extent = 0;
    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
    }
    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
        }
        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
            ? local + std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank]
            : local;
        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
        if (scatter) {
            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        } else {
            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, const int rank, const int ranks) {
    Options options;
    int parseStatus = rank == 0 ? parseOptions(argc, argv, options) : 0;
    MPI_Bcast(&parseStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (parseStatus != 0) return parseStatus == 2 ? 0 : 1;

    index_t parameters[3] = {options.numRows, options.sparsity, options.iterations};
    int flags[2] = {options.validate, options.printResults};
    MPI_Bcast(parameters, 3, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(&options.maxVal, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(flags, 2, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const index_t numRows = parameters[0];
    const index_t sparsity = parameters[1];
    const index_t iterations = parameters[2];
    const double maxVal = options.maxVal;
    const bool validate = flags[0];
    const bool printResults = flags[1];
    const uint64_t entries = static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
    const index_t nItems = static_cast<index_t>(entries / sparsity);

    // The original random sequence is generated once, on rank zero only.
    // Worker ranks never allocate the global matrix or reference solution.
    std::vector<double> h_val, h_reference, h_out;
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols, h_rowDelimiters;
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);
    std::vector<index_t> rowCuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
    std::vector<index_t> nnzCuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
        printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
        printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
        printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", nItems,
               entries ? 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / entries) : 100.0);
        printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
        printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("MPI processes: %d\n", ranks);
        printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
        h_val.resize(nItems);
        h_cols.resize(nItems);
        h_rowDelimiters.resize(static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1);
        // MPI initialization may use rand(); reproduce the C library's default seed.
        srand(1);
        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

        if (validate) {
            printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
            h_reference.resize(numRows);
            spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
                    h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
        }
        if (printResults || validate) h_out.resize(numRows);
        rowCuts = partitionRows(h_rowDelimiters, numRows, ranks);
        for (int p = 0; p <= ranks; ++p) nnzCuts[p] = h_rowDelimiters[rowCuts[p]];
    }

    // The input vector is immutable across iterations: broadcast it once.
    // Use chunks for vectors larger than MPI's int count range as well.
    const uint64_t maxCount = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
    for (uint64_t offset = 0; offset < numRows; offset += maxCount) {
        MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data() + offset,
                  static_cast<int>(std::min(maxCount, static_cast<uint64_t>(numRows) - offset)),
                  MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
    MPI_Bcast(rowCuts.data(), ranks + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(nnzCuts.data(), ranks + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    const index_t localRows = rowCuts[rank + 1] - rowCuts[rank];
    const index_t localNnz = nnzCuts[rank + 1] - nnzCuts[rank];
    std::vector<double> localVal(localNnz), localOut(localRows);
    std::vector<index_t> localCols(localNnz), localRowDelimiters(static_cast<size_t>(localRows) + 1);
    std::vector<uint64_t> rowSizes(ranks), nnzSizes(ranks);
    for (int p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
        rowSizes[p] = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowCuts[p + 1]) - rowCuts[p];
        nnzSizes[p] = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnzCuts[p + 1]) - nnzCuts[p];
    }
    transferSlices(h_val.data(), localVal.data(), nnzCuts, nnzSizes, MPI_DOUBLE, rank, true);
    transferSlices(h_cols.data(), localCols.data(), nnzCuts, nnzSizes, MPI_UINT32_T, rank, true);
    transferSlices(h_rowDelimiters.data(), localRowDelimiters.data(), rowCuts, rowSizes,
                   MPI_UINT32_T, rank, true);
    for (index_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
        localRowDelimiters[row] -= nnzCuts[rank];
    }
    localRowDelimiters.back() = localNnz;

    // Release root's global CSR before timing; every rank now owns only its slice.
    std::vector<double>().swap(h_val);
    std::vector<index_t>().swap(h_cols);
    std::vector<index_t>().swap(h_rowDelimiters);

    if (rank == 0) printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        spmvCpu(localVal.data(), localCols.data(), localRowDelimiters.data(),
                h_vec.data(), localRows, localOut.data());
    }

    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    // Assemble the final vector only when it is observable. There is no
    // communication in the repeated SpMV loop, including with validation enabled.
    if (printResults || validate) {
        transferSlices(h_out.data(), localOut.data(), rowCuts, rowSizes, MPI_DOUBLE, rank, false);
    }

    int status = 0;
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
        const double gflops = duration > 0.0 ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / duration / 1e9 : 0.0;
        const double avgTime = iterations ? duration * 1000.0 / iterations : 0.0;
        printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
        if (printResults) print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
        if (validate) {
            printf("Validating result...\n");
            const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);
            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
            status = valid ? 0 : 1;
        }
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    return status;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
    int status = 0;
    try {
        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        return 1;
    }
    MPI_Finalize();
    return status;
}

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(spmv PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# MPI is required even for a one-process run.
target_link_libraries(spmv PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)

def run(cmd):
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=45)
    assert p.returncode == 0, (cmd, p.returncode, p.stdout, p.stderr)

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    errno = 0;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (text[0] == '-' || end == text || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;
+}

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

codex
The MPI version builds, and the initial checks match the original results and hashes exactly. Each rank now holds only its CSR partition during computation; results are gathered once when printing or validation requires them. I’m checking sparse and empty partitions, edge cases, and performance across rank counts.

/bin/bash -lc "mpicxx -std=c++20 -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra -DOMPI_SKIP_MPICXX=1 -DMPICH_SKIP_MPICXX=1 spmv/spmv.cpp -o /tmp/spmv-mpi-performance && python3 - <<'PY'
import re
import subprocess

args = ['-n', '4096', '-s', '10', '-i', '300', '-v', '-r']
reference = subprocess.run(['/tmp/spmv-original', *args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, timeout=60).stdout
expected = re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*?=== END RESULTS ===', reference, re.S).group(0)
for ranks in (1, 2, 4, 8, 16):
    p = subprocess.run(['mpirun', '--oversubscribe', '--bind-to', 'core', '--map-by', 'core', '-np', str(ranks), '/tmp/spmv-mpi-performance', *args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, timeout=60)
    assert expected == re.search(r'=== RESULTS ===.*?=== END RESULTS ===', p.stdout, re.S).group(0), (ranks, p.stdout)
    assert 'Validation: PASSED' in p.stdout
    duration = re.search(r'Computation time: ([\\d.]+) ms', p.stdout).group(1)
    gflops = re.search(r'Performance: ([\\d.]+) GFLOPS', p.stdout).group(1)
    print(f'{ranks} ranks: {duration} ms, {gflops} GFLOPS; exact serial output and validation passed', flush=True)

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t& value) {

+    errno = 0;

+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Invalid integer argument: %s\n", text);
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);

+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
+    int status = 0;
+    try {
+        status = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, ranks);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;

+    const uint64_t nItems = static_cast<uint64_t>(options.numRows) * options.numRows
+                          / options.sparsity;
+    if (nItems > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        printf("Too many non-zero elements for 32-bit CSR indices.\n");
+        return 1;
+    }
+    return 0;
+}
 
-    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
-    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;
+// Partition complete rows by CSR work, including overhead for empty rows.
+// Never split a row: each dot product retains the original addition order.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows, const int ranks) {
+    std::vector<index_t> cuts(static_cast<size_t>(ranks) + 1);
+    cuts.back() = numRows;
+    const uint64_t work = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters.back()) + numRows;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < ranks; ++rank) {
+        const uint64_t target = work * static_cast<uint64_t>(rank) / ranks;
+        index_t lo = cuts[rank - 1];
+        index_t hi = numRows;
+        while (lo < hi) {
+            const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;
+            if (static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid < target) {
+                lo = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                hi = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        if (lo > cuts[rank - 1]) {
+            const uint64_t before = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo - 1]) + lo - 1;
+            const uint64_t after = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[lo]) + lo;
+            if (target - before <= after - target) --lo;
+        }
+        cuts[rank] = lo;
+    }
+    return cuts;
+}
+
+// Scatter/gather contiguous slices in collective windows so that MPI's int
+// counts/displacements do not limit the total matrix to INT_MAX nonzeros.
+template <typename T>
+void transferSlices(T* global, T* local, const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                    const std::vector<uint64_t>& sizes, MPI_Datatype datatype,
+                    const int rank, const bool scatter) {
+    const size_t ranks = sizes.size();
+    uint64_t extent = 0;
+    for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+        extent = std::max(extent, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+    }
+    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), displacements(ranks);
+    const uint64_t windowSize = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();
+    for (uint64_t window = 0; window < extent; window += windowSize) {
+        const uint64_t windowEnd = std::min(extent, window + windowSize);
+        for (size_t p = 0; p < ranks; ++p) {
+            const uint64_t begin = std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]));
+            const uint64_t end = std::min(windowEnd, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[p]) + sizes[p]);
+            counts[p] = end > begin ? static_cast<int>(end - begin) : 0;
+            displacements[p] = counts[p] ? static_cast<int>(begin - window) : 0;
+        }
+        T* localWindow = counts[rank]
+            ? local + (std::max(window, static_cast<uint64_t>(starts[rank])) - starts[rank])
+            : local;
+        T* globalWindow = rank == 0 ? global + window : nullptr;
+        if (scatter) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(globalWindow, counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype,
+                         localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(localWindow, counts[rank], datatype, globalWindow,
+                        counts.data(), displacements.data(), datatype, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+    }
+}

The tested cases match the serial program exactly, including empty rows and configurations with more ranks than rows. MPI error exits, validation failures, and sanitizer checks also passed. For a 4096-row matrix over 300 iterations, the computation took about 764 ms on one rank and 49 ms on 16 ranks; initialization and MPI startup are excluded from those timings.
